# Skills · M · part 11

805 entries, maister:mockup-studio to make-character. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **maister:mockup-studio**: Generates UI mockups (rendered HTML/CSS via a browser visual companion, or terminal ASCII). Discovers and binds to the project's frontend/UI/UX resources (standards, design system, available design skills). Invoked standalone by users, or by the development and product-design orchestrators as their mockup-generation step. ([SkillPanel/maister](https://github.com/SkillPanel/maister/tree/HEAD/plugins/maister/skills/mockup-studio) · ★268)
- **maister:performance**: Orchestrates performance optimization workflows using static code analysis to identify bottlenecks (N+1 queries, missing indexes, O(n^2) algorithms, blocking I/O, memory leaks). Accepts optional user-provided profiling data. Reuses standard specification, planning, implementation, and verification phases. ([SkillPanel/maister](https://github.com/SkillPanel/maister/tree/HEAD/plugins/maister/skills/performance) · ★268 · 2 copies)
- **maister:problem-classifier**: Classify business requirements into one of 4 modeling problem classes (CRUD, Transformation & Presentation, Integration, Resource Contention). Runs a signal scan, asks targeted clarifying questions, and recommends an implementation approach with rationale. NOT an archetype — invoke when the user asks about modeling problem classes, "jaka klasa problemu", "jak to sklasyfikować modelarsko", "problem class", or similar. For archetypes (accounting, pricing), use the *-archetype-mapper skills instead. ([Architekt-Jutra/architekt-jutra-code](https://github.com/Architekt-Jutra/architekt-jutra-code/tree/HEAD/week8/3/problem-classifier) · ★57 · 2 copies)
- **maister:product-design**: Interactive product/feature design orchestrator. ([SkillPanel/maister](https://github.com/SkillPanel/maister/tree/HEAD/plugins/maister/skills/product-design) · ★268 · 2 copies)

- **majesticway-task-creation**: &gt; Load this file when asked to create new benchmark tasks using the MajesticWay project dataset. &gt; For the **sj-majestic** benchmark, place tasks under `C:\Users\robwa\Documents\Skills-Sandbox\Benchmark\sj-majestic\tasks\`. &gt; For the **aec-bench** benchmark, place tasks under `C:\Users\robwa\Documents\Skills-Sandbox\Benchmark\aec-bench\tasks\`. &gt; Read `CLAUDE.md` in the aec-bench project root befo ([robikscube/SJ-skills-sandbox](https://github.com/robikscube/SJ-skills-sandbox/tree/HEAD/Skill-List/majesticway-task-creation))
- **maji-commit**: Generate a concise commit message from the current git diff. Conventional commits format (feat/fix/refactor/etc). Use when staged changes are ready to commit and you want Claude to write the message instead of typing it manually. ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-commit) · ★19)
- **maji-debug**: Disciplined debug session protocol. Read symptom → form hypothesis → verify before fixing → propose minimum-change fix. Pre-Action Gate prevents hasty rewrites that break unrelated code. Use when an error or unexpected behaviour appears and you want a focused diagnosis instead of guess-and-check. ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-debug) · ★19)
- **maji-decision-log**: Keep an append-only decision log for a project. Capture each non-obvious choice with its context, what was chosen, what was rejected and why; answer "why did we choose X?" from the log; record reversals as new entries instead of edits. Use when a trade-off is settled in a session, when someone asks why something is the way it is, or before reopening a choice that was already made. ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-decision-log) · ★19)
- **maji-doc**: Generate concise documentation from code. README, function docs, API references, architecture notes — written in plain language, with examples that work, no padding. Use when shipping or onboarding and you want docs that read fast and stay accurate. ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-doc) · ★19)
- **maji-explain**: Concise code or concept explainer. Lead with one-sentence answer, then layered detail only if useful. No padding, no "let me walk you through". Use when encountering unfamiliar code, library, error message, or concept and you want fast comprehension instead of a textbook lecture. ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-explain) · ★19)
- **maji-jimat**: Token-economy mode for any coding agent. Strip filler, cap verbose replies, and switch on graduated compression (ringan / penuh / ultra) or the sibling modes dry / answer-only. Fires when the user types "jimat on/off", "jimat ringan/penuh/ultra", "dry on/off", "answer-only on/off", or a per-message "/jimat" prefix. Preserves code, file paths, and accuracy. Also covers agentic token economy: extracting before fan-out, batching items per subagent, pipelines without barriers, and durable state, for when "the workflow burns too many tokens" or "we hit the usage limit". ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-jimat) · ★19)
- **maji-mode**: A Claude collaboration discipline. Lead with outcome+constraint, identify decision modes (discuss/build/pause/pivot), recognize frustration signals, enforce a Pre-Action Gate before risky operations. Use when starting any session to ship with less rework and scope drift. ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-mode) · ★19)
- **maji-post-mortem**: Write a blameless post-mortem after something went wrong (failed deploy, broken tests, data loss, leaked secret, a dead-end approach that cost hours): timeline, impact, root cause, what helped, what hurt, and concrete action items with owners. Appends to a project log and reminds you of past lessons when work touches the same area again. Use when the user says "post-mortem", "what went wrong", "log this failure", or after an incident or a wasted afternoon. ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-post-mortem) · ★19)
- **maji-refactor**: Scope-guarded refactoring. Define target → identify minimum diff → preserve behaviour → verify with tests. Pre-Action Gate prevents scope drift into unrelated improvements. Use when you want a targeted refactor that does not turn into a 50-file rewrite. ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-refactor) · ★19)
- **maji-responsive-audit**: Audit and fix a web page or component for every screen size. Checks mobile-first layout, dynamic viewport units, safe-area insets, 44px touch targets, fluid type, container queries, responsive images, horizontal overflow and mobile form inputs at 320, 375, 768, 1024 and 1440 px, then reports findings by severity with file:line and the fix. Use when asked to "make this responsive", "fix the mobile layout", "check it on phone", or before shipping a new page. ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-responsive-audit) · ★19)
- **maji-review**: Disciplined code review skill. Outputs structured feedback grouped by severity (critical/important/style/suggestion) with file:line references. No preamble, no padding, evidence-first. Use when reviewing a PR, diff, or file and you want focused actionable feedback instead of a wall of comments. ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-review) · ★19)
- **maji-setup**: First-run setup for a MAJI Skills agent folder. Interview the user in short rounds, write me/profile.md from profile.example.md, create me/overrides/ and me/learned.md, then report which skills now have what they need. Also updates an existing profile section by section. Use when the user says "run maji-setup", "set me up", "set up my agent", "update my profile", "my stack changed", "change my brand", or in Malay "jom setup", "setup agent aku", "kemaskini profile aku". Run it too when a skill finds me/profile.md missing and the user agrees. ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-setup) · ★19)
- **maji-summary**: Compress long content into structured summary. TLDR + key points + action items, length-tunable. Use for meeting notes, transcripts, long docs, threads, codebases. No padding, no editorial commentary. ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-summary) · ★19)
- **maji-test**: Generate disciplined test cases. Focus on behaviour not implementation, cover edge cases without bloat, name tests for the case not the function. Use when writing tests for new code or filling gaps in coverage. ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-test) · ★19)
- **maji-todo**: Extract and triage TODO/FIXME/HACK comments from a codebase. Categorize by urgency, group by file, identify stale TODOs older than threshold. Use during cleanup sprints, before releases, or when onboarding a new project. ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-todo) · ★19)
- **maji-typescript-strict**: TypeScript type-safety discipline for agents editing .ts and .tsx files. Never write `as any`; reach for a type guard, a schema parse at the boundary, `satisfies`, `unknown`, or an exhaustive `never` check instead; keep unavoidable casts inside one typed wrapper. Also audits a file or folder for existing `any`, unsafe casts and non-null assertions, and fixes them in order of risk. Use whenever writing TypeScript, when type errors are being silenced, or when asked to "clean up the types" or "remove the any". ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-typescript-strict) · ★19)
- **majia-chatcut-koubo**: ChatCut 口播与录屏视频的一句话编排和安全默认层。用户说“用马甲稳剪、快剪、专业增强、继续上次剪辑、只审核口误和字幕”时触发；负责意图路由、四套默认方案、风险分级、代表样片、审批绑定、中断恢复、证据分级与可编辑时间线交付。项目操作与工具参数始终交给 ChatCut 官方 Skill。默认不重排观点，不添加音乐、动态图形、补充画面或生成素材，不导出、不发布。真实 ChatCut 端到端只有当前匿名样本和工具证据齐全时才能宣称通过。 ([adunext/human-video-knowledge](https://github.com/adunext/human-video-knowledge/tree/HEAD/upstream/maojiebc__majia-chatcut-koubo) · ★56 · 2 copies)
- **majia-guanyuan**: 观远 BI（Guandata）实战增益层。标准查数、指标批量查询、本地分析、建卡发布、ETL、数据集和表单结构管理、工作流、指标写入路由官方六组件。专攻 ETL 整库治理、SmartETL 全链路重写、引擎报错、自定义图表与 HTML 看板排障、看板口径档案与只读逐格验收、v7 发布兼容、移动端 phoneLayout、门店手机成绩单、SuperApp 的 LLM 中转及历史兼容、AI-native ADS 架构判断。餐饮会员公式见 majia-huiyuan。触发：execute-sql 5001、SQL 直查不可用、ETL 预览汇总、分块取数、历史数据抽取、预览截断、数字出处、看板口径档案、看板答案对账、观远、Guandata、会员、订单、复购率、RFM、ETL 治理、payload_json、自定义图表、HTML 看板、门店手机成绩单、换店滤空、phoneLayout、60004、SuperApp、ILLEGAL_JSON_RES、数据架构。 ([maojiebc/majia-guanyuan](https://github.com/maojiebc/majia-guanyuan/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★18)
- **majia-huiyuan**: 会员数据顾问·马甲实战版（majia-huiyuan）。当核心交付物是会员指标口径、RFM、复购/留存/流失公式、核销率、客单价、会员分层/分群、人群圈选、标签体系、CDP、OneID 身份打通、Cohort、CRM/私域数据分析、会员数仓（DIM/DWD/DWS/ADS）、SQL/DDL、字段词典、数据质量、会员看板或观远 BI 复刻时使用。用户提出召回、提频、防流失、流失预警、新客转化、渠道迁移（外卖↔堂食）、导购任务分派等会员运营动作时，动作背后的数据依据（圈谁/何时/力度/派给谁/怎么回收）由本 Skill 负责；动作的执行内容（朋友圈、群发、欢迎语、社群 SOP、企微操作）与私域整盘经营诊断走 majia-siyu——同一动作的两半，先数据后执行。全部数值为模拟数据，仅结构与口径可引用。 ([maojiebc/majia-huiyuan](https://github.com/maojiebc/majia-huiyuan/tree/HEAD/skillhub/majia-huiyuan) · ★14 · 2 copies)
- **majia-match-the-venue**: Match The Venue（MTV）— 给国内城市的"高端培训/商学院/咨询/私董会"事件匹配最合适的酒店宴会厅，靠公开活动证据导航。喂活动链接（微信公众号/活动家/活动行/任意 URL）即可抽取"哪家机构 在 哪家酒店 办了什么课"，入库打分后输出选址推荐报告。当用户提到"找培训酒店"、"高管培训场地"、"会场推荐"、"宴会厅选址"、"培训选址"、"高端培训去哪开"、"match the venue"、"majia-match-the-venue"、"/mtv" 时触发。首发覆盖上海 6 个区（浦东/静安/黄浦/徐汇/长宁/闵行虹桥），跟踪 19 家高端机构（长江/中欧/得到/混沌/麦肯锡/贝恩/BCG/正和岛等）。不做酒店预订、不做询价撮合，只做"前人选址经验证据库"。 ([maojiebc/majia-match-the-venue](https://github.com/maojiebc/majia-match-the-venue/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★2)
- **majia-meeting-svg**: 将会议逐字稿或录音转写文本转化为手机端可直接转发的 SVG 会议纪要卡片，并自动转为 PNG 方便分享。产出双方/多方都能用的结构化可视摘要。当用户提供会议录音转写、逐字稿、会议记录并要求生成会议纪要、会议摘要、meeting minutes、会议框架图、SVG 纪要时使用此技能。即使用户只是说「帮我整理一下这个会议」「把这段对话做个纪要」「会议总结一下」，也应触发。 ([maojiebc/majia-meeting-svg](https://github.com/maojiebc/majia-meeting-svg/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★3)
- **majia-siyu**: 私域专家团 · 马甲实战版（majia-siyu）。处理朋友圈、群发、欢迎语、企微引流、私域诊断、整盘搭建、厂商选型/竞品/报价/市场地图、客户档案与任务后导航。门店拉新、加盟店推不动、总部还是门店发、企微任务转发和加好友、台卡、码放哪、先进群还是先加好友、推送前准备、效果怎么算、分层召回、企微从零搭，这些有马甲的真实案例，先读本入口再答。涉及厂商、产品、价格、功能、案例、政策、平台规则或公司存续时，必须实时联网核验并附证据；不能联网时不得输出具体动态事实。 边界：会员指标口径、RFM、复购/留存公式、SQL/DDL、数仓、字段词典、数据质量、会员看板这类数据交付物不触发本 Skill，改用 majia-huiyuan，召回/提频/防流失的数据依据也在那边；模糊的私域经营问题先由本入口诊断，出现“怎么算、口径、SQL、表、看板、数据核验”才转 majia-huiyuan。 ([maojiebc/majia-siyu-team](https://github.com/maojiebc/majia-siyu-team/tree/HEAD/plugins/siyu-core/skills/majia-siyu) · ★3 · 2 copies)

- **major-learning-coach**: 按用户所在专业提供"从简到难"的学习路径、知识点讲解与解题辅导：先确认专业， 再诊断基础定起点，然后按先修顺序逐点推进，每个知识点都给可检验的学习目标、 L1/L2/L3 分档例题与自测题。当用户说「我是XX专业的」「我想学XX」「这道题怎么做」 「从零开始学」「先学什么后学什么」「帮我规划学习路线」「帮我复习/查漏」 「测测我什么水平」，或提到任何大学专业名、课程名、教材名时使用本技能。 Use this skill whenever the user names their major or a university course or textbook, asks how to solve a homework or exam problem, wants a learning roadmap from beginner to advanced, or asks what to learn first in a discipline. ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach))
- **major-logic**: 逻辑学（logic）的学习路径与解题辅导：判断一个论证是否有效、写自然演绎证明、 把自然语言译成一阶公式、证明集合的可数性与基数。训练用形式语言把推理拆成可检查的 步骤，指出论证在哪一步跳过了前提。当用户提到逻辑、数理逻辑、 形式逻辑、符号逻辑、模态逻辑、集合论、批判性思维，或问真值表与归谬、自然演绎、 谓词翻译、可数性证明、哥德尔不完备性时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/logic))
- **major-marketing**: 市场营销（marketing）的学习路径与解题辅导：STP 方案设计、市场调研设计、定价与渠道计算、案例诊断。 训练在预算与合规约束下回答"服务谁、凭什么被选、怎么被记住"，并把答案落成 4P 组合。 当用户提到营销、市场、市营、品牌、市场营销学、消费者行为学、市场调研、渠道管理，或问 STP 怎么做、 4P 怎么配、问卷怎么设计、价格弹性怎么算时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/marketing))
- **major-matching-and-explanation**: Ranks the top 3 majors from catalog.json for a student profile and explains each fit with evidence from the profile and the catalog. ([GRAIL-innovationai/grail-hackathon](https://github.com/GRAIL-innovationai/grail-hackathon/tree/HEAD/submissions/montana-state-2026/advisors-t/skills/major-matching-and-explanation) · ★1)
- **major-materials-science**: 材料科学与工程（materials-science）的学习路径与解题辅导：相图与组织计算、强度与硬度校核、 工艺—组织—性能推理、表征结果判读。训练的是用成分—工艺—结构—性能四链条解释材料行为。 当用户提到材料、材科、金属材料、无机非金属、高分子，或问杠杆定律、CCT/TTT、 Hall-Petch、XRD 峰位、SEM/TEM 判读时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/materials-science))
- **major-mathematics**: 数学与应用数学（mathematics）的学习路径与解题辅导：从定义出发写证明、用极限与展开求值、 判断收敛性并构造反例。当用户提到数学、数应、应数、数学系、数分、高代、实变、泛函， 或问证明题怎么写、极限与积分怎么算、级数与反常积分是否收敛、某个结论的反例在哪、 这个条件能不能去掉时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/mathematics))
- **major-mechanical-engineering**: 机械工程（mechanical-engineering）的学习路径与解题辅导：受力分析与平衡、内力图与强度校核、 机构自由度与运动分析。当用户提到机械、机电、机制、机设、理力、材力，或问受力图怎么画、 弯矩图怎么画、强度怎么校核、自由度怎么数、轴承与齿轮怎么选型时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/mechanical-engineering))

- **major-museology**: 文物与博物馆学（museology）的学习路径与解题辅导：判断文物价值、设计展览叙事、做藏品保存决策。 它训练的是把一件实物同时放进价值框架、叙事框架与风险框架，留下别人能复核的判断与方案。 当用户提到文博、博物馆、文物、文物学概论、博物馆学、藏品管理、文物保护技术， 或问文物价值评估、展陈设计方案、保护方案评估、文博案例分析时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/museology))
- **major-musicology**: 音乐学（musicology）的学习路径与解题辅导：和声功能分析、曲式与作品分析、 听辨描述、音乐史论述。训练的是从谱面与听觉读出结构，并说清"为什么这里好听"。 当用户提到音乐学、音乐、音学、音乐教育、乐理、视唱练耳、和声、曲式、 中国音乐史，或问和声功能怎么标、曲式怎么划分、听辨报告怎么写、史论怎么组织时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/musicology))
- **major-nursing**: 护理学（nursing）的学习路径与解题辅导：护理程序五步、基础护理操作、内外科与急危重症护理、社区与健康教育。 它训练用评估—诊断—计划—实施—评价的闭环照护病人，把病人的反应变成可观察、可交接的指标。 当用户提到护理、护专、护理学、助产、基础护理学、内科护理学，或问护理措施题、案例分析题、操作流程题、健康教育题时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/nursing))

- **major-purchase-decision**: Applies a structured buy/wait/skip decision framework to any large purchase. Evaluates affordability, needs vs. wants, financing cost, opportunity cost, and timing.
